<title></title>
<text>
## Example usage

In the [DuckDB CLI ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/stable/clients/cli/overview.html) (Command Line Interface), run the following commands:

```

-- Install the iceberg DuckDB extension (if you haven't already) and load the extension.

INSTALL iceberg;

LOAD iceberg;


-- Install and load httpfs extension for reading/writing files over HTTP(S).

INSTALL httpfs;

LOAD httpfs;


-- Create a DuckDB secret to store R2 Data Catalog credentials.

CREATE SECRET r2_secret (

    TYPE ICEBERG,

    TOKEN '<token>'

);


-- Attach R2 Data Catalog with the following ATTACH statement.

ATTACH '<warehouse_name>' AS my_r2_catalog (

    TYPE ICEBERG,

    ENDPOINT '<catalog_uri>'

);


-- Create the default schema in the catalog and set it as the active schema.

CREATE SCHEMA my_r2_catalog.default;

USE my_r2_catalog.default;


-- Create and populate a sample Iceberg table with data.

CREATE TABLE my_iceberg_table AS SELECT a FROM range(4) t(a);


-- Show all available tables.

SHOW ALL TABLES;


-- Query the Iceberg table you just created.

SELECT * FROM my_r2_catalog.default.my_iceberg_table;


```

```json
{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"BreadcrumbList","itemListElement":[{"@type":"ListItem","position":1,"item":{"@id":"/directory/","name":"Directory"}},{"@type":"ListItem","position":2,"item":{"@id":"/r2/","name":"R2"}},{"@type":"ListItem","position":3,"item":{"@id":"/r2/data-catalog/","name":"R2 Data Catalog"}},{"@type":"ListItem","position":4,"item":{"@id":"/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/","name":"Connect to Iceberg engines"}},{"@type":"ListItem","position":5,"item":{"@id":"/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/duckdb/","name":"DuckDB"}}]}
```
# DuckDB

**Last reviewed:**  10 months ago 

Below is an example of using [DuckDB ↗](https://duckdb.org/) to connect to R2 Data Catalog. For more information on connecting to R2 Data Catalog with DuckDB, refer to [DuckDB documentation ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/stable/core%5Fextensions/iceberg/iceberg%5Frest%5Fcatalogs#r2-catalog).

## Prerequisites

* Sign up for a [Cloudflare account ↗](https://dash.cloudflare.com/sign-up/workers-and-pages).
* [Create an R2 bucket](/r2/buckets/create-buckets/) and [enable the data catalog](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/#enable-r2-data-catalog-on-a-bucket).
* [Create an R2 API token](/r2/api/tokens/) with both [R2 and data catalog permissions](/r2/api/tokens/#permissions).
* Install [DuckDB ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/installation/).  
   * Note: [DuckDB 1.4.0 ↗](https://github.com/duckdb/duckdb/releases/tag/v1.4.0) or greater is required to attach and write to [Iceberg REST Catalogs ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/stable/core%5Fextensions/iceberg/iceberg%5Frest%5Fcatalogs).
* Note: DuckDB [does not currently support ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/stable/core%5Fextensions/iceberg/iceberg%5Frest%5Fcatalogs#limitations-for-update-and-delete) `DELETE` on partitioned tables.

## Example usage

In the [DuckDB CLI ↗](https://duckdb.org/docs/stable/clients/cli/overview.html) (Command Line Interface), run the following commands:

```

-- Install the iceberg DuckDB extension (if you haven't already) and load the extension.

INSTALL iceberg;

LOAD iceberg;

<title></title>
<text>
# Complex types

R2 SQL supports querying struct, array, and map column types stored in Iceberg tables. This page covers access patterns, supported functions, and examples for each type.

---

## Structs

Struct columns contain named fields. Access fields using bracket notation or the `get_field()` function.

### Bracket notation

```

SELECT pricing['price'] AS price,

       pricing['discount_percent'] AS discount

FROM my_namespace.products

LIMIT 5


```

### get\_field function

```

SELECT get_field(pricing, 'price') AS price,

       get_field(pricing, 'discount_percent') AS discount

FROM my_namespace.products

LIMIT 5


```

### Struct fields in WHERE

```

SELECT customer_id, pricing['price'] AS price

FROM my_namespace.products

WHERE pricing['price'] > 50

LIMIT 10


```

### Struct fields in ORDER BY

```

SELECT customer_id, pricing['price'] AS price

FROM my_namespace.products

WHERE pricing['price'] IS NOT NULL

ORDER BY pricing['price'] DESC

LIMIT 10


```

### Struct fields in GROUP BY

```

SELECT platforms['windows'] AS windows_support,

       COUNT(*) AS product_count,

       AVG(pricing['price']) AS avg_price

FROM my_namespace.products

WHERE pricing['price'] IS NOT NULL

GROUP BY platforms['windows']


```

### Creating structs inline

```

-- named_struct creates a struct with named fields

SELECT named_struct('id', customer_id, 'amount', total_amount) AS info

FROM my_namespace.sales_data

WHERE total_amount IS NOT NULL

LIMIT 1


-- struct creates a struct with positional fields

SELECT struct(customer_id, total_amount, region) AS info

FROM my_namespace.sales_data

WHERE total_amount IS NOT NULL

LIMIT 1


```

---

## Arrays

Array columns contain ordered lists of values. Array indexing is **1-based**.

### Index access

```

SELECT customer_id, tags[1] AS first_tag, tags[2] AS second_tag

FROM my_namespace.products

LIMIT 5


```

### Create arrays

#### make\_array

Creates an array from a list of values.

```


</text>

<title></title>
<text>
## 7\. Query your data using R2 SQL

Set up your environment to use R2 SQL:

Terminal window

```

export WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Or create a `.env` file with:

```

WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN


```

Where `YOUR_API_TOKEN` is the token you created in step 3\. For more information on setting environment variables, refer to [Wrangler system environment variables](/workers/wrangler/system-environment-variables/).

Query your data:

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 sql query "YOUR_WAREHOUSE_NAME" "

SELECT

    user_id,

    event_type,

    product_id,

    amount

FROM default.ecommerce

WHERE event_type = 'purchase'

LIMIT 10"


```

Replace `YOUR_WAREHOUSE_NAME` with the warehouse name from step 2.

You can also query this table with any engine that supports Apache Iceberg. To learn more about connecting other engines to R2 Data Catalog, refer to [Connect to Iceberg engines](/r2/data-catalog/config-examples/).

## Learn more

[ Managing R2 Data Catalogs ](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/) Enable or disable R2 Data Catalog on your bucket, retrieve configuration details, and authenticate your Iceberg engine.

<title></title>
<text>
## Example usage

In your Snowflake [SQL worksheet ↗](https://docs.snowflake.com/en/user-guide/ui-snowsight-worksheets-gs) or [notebook ↗](https://docs.snowflake.com/en/user-guide/ui-snowsight/notebooks), run the following commands:

```

-- Create a database (if you don't already have one) to organize your external data

CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS r2_example_db;


-- Create an external volume pointing to your R2 bucket

CREATE OR REPLACE EXTERNAL VOLUME ext_vol_r2

    STORAGE_LOCATIONS = (

        (

            NAME = 'my_r2_storage_location'

            STORAGE_PROVIDER = 'S3COMPAT'

            STORAGE_BASE_URL = 's3compat://<bucket-name>'

            CREDENTIALS = (

                AWS_KEY_ID = '<access_key>'

                AWS_SECRET_KEY = '<secret_access_key>'

            )

            STORAGE_ENDPOINT = '<account_id>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com'

        )

    )

    ALLOW_WRITES = FALSE;


-- Create a catalog integration for R2 Data Catalog (read-only)

CREATE OR REPLACE CATALOG INTEGRATION r2_data_catalog

    CATALOG_SOURCE = ICEBERG_REST

    TABLE_FORMAT = ICEBERG

    CATALOG_NAMESPACE = 'default'

    REST_CONFIG = (

        CATALOG_URI = '<catalog_uri>'

        CATALOG_NAME = '<warehouse_name>'

    )

    REST_AUTHENTICATION = (

        TYPE = BEARER

        BEARER_TOKEN = '<token>'

    )

    ENABLED = TRUE;


-- Create an Apache Iceberg table in your selected Snowflake database

CREATE ICEBERG TABLE my_iceberg_table

    CATALOG = 'r2_data_catalog'

    EXTERNAL_VOLUME = 'ext_vol_r2'

    CATALOG_TABLE_NAME = 'my_table';  -- Name of existing table in your R2 data catalog


-- Query your Iceberg table

SELECT * FROM my_iceberg_table;


```

R2 SQL

![hero image](/_astro/hero.CVYJHPAd_26AMqX.svg) 

Mar 23, 2026
1. ### [R2 SQL now supports over 190 new functions, expressions, and complex types](/changelog/post/2026-03-23-expanded-sql-functions-expressions-complex-types/)  
[ R2 SQL ](/r2-sql/)  
[R2 SQL](/r2-sql/) now supports an expanded SQL grammar so you can write richer analytical queries without exporting data. This release adds CASE expressions, column aliases, arithmetic in clauses, 163 scalar functions, 33 aggregate functions, EXPLAIN, Common Table Expressions (CTEs),and full struct/array/map access. R2 SQL is Cloudflare's serverless, distributed, analytics query engine for querying [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables stored in [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). This page documents the supported SQL syntax.  
#### Highlights  
   * **Column aliases** — `SELECT col AS alias` now works in all clauses  
   * **CASE expressions** — conditional logic directly in SQL (searched and simple forms)

# Query data

Query [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables managed by [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). R2 SQL queries can be made via [Wrangler](/workers/wrangler/) or HTTP API.

## Get your warehouse name

To query data with R2 SQL, you'll need your warehouse name associated with your [catalog](/r2/data-catalog/manage-catalogs/). To retrieve it, you can run the [r2 bucket catalog get command](/workers/wrangler/commands/r2/#r2-bucket-catalog-get):

Terminal window

```

npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog get <BUCKET_NAME>


```

Alternatively, you can find it in the dashboard by going to the **R2 object storage** page, selecting the bucket, switching to the **Settings** tab, scrolling to **R2 Data Catalog**, and finding **Warehouse name**.

## Query via Wrangler

To begin, install [npm ↗](https://docs.npmjs.com/getting-started). Then [install Wrangler, the Developer Platform CLI](/workers/wrangler/install-and-update/).


## Query via API

Below is an example of using R2 SQL via the REST endpoint:

Terminal window

```

curl -X POST \

  "https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/{ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/{BUCKET_NAME}" \

  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN}" \

  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \

  -d '{

    "query": "SELECT * FROM namespace.table_name limit 10;"

  }'


```

The API requires an API token with the appropriate permissions in the Authorization header. Refer to [Authentication](#authentication) for details on creating a token.

For a full list of supported SQL commands, refer to the [R2 SQL reference](/r2-sql/sql-reference/).

## Authentication

To query data with R2 SQL, you must provide a Cloudflare API token with R2 SQL, R2 Data Catalog, and R2 storage permissions. R2 SQL requires these permissions to access catalog metadata and read the underlying data files stored in R2.

### Create API token in the dashboard

1. ### [R2 SQL now supports over 190 new functions, expressions, and complex types](/changelog/post/2026-03-23-expanded-sql-functions-expressions-complex-types/)  
[ R2 SQL ](/r2-sql/)  
[R2 SQL](/r2-sql/) now supports an expanded SQL grammar so you can write richer analytical queries without exporting data. This release adds CASE expressions, column aliases, arithmetic in clauses, 163 scalar functions, 33 aggregate functions, EXPLAIN, Common Table Expressions (CTEs),and full struct/array/map access. R2 SQL is Cloudflare's serverless, distributed, analytics query engine for querying [Apache Iceberg ↗](https://iceberg.apache.org/) tables stored in [R2 Data Catalog](/r2/data-catalog/). This page documents the supported SQL syntax.  
#### Highlights  
   * **Column aliases** — `SELECT col AS alias` now works in all clauses  
   * **CASE expressions** — conditional logic directly in SQL (searched and simple forms)

良い質問です。今回の設計で `streaming.linear_events` テーブルに入ったデータは、以下のようにクエリできます。

---

#### R2 SQL（Wrangler CLI）

```bash
# 環境変数設定
export WRANGLER_R2_SQL_AUTH_TOKEN=YOUR_API_TOKEN

# 全イベント確認
npx wrangler r2 sql query "lake" "
  SELECT * FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 10
"

# Issue の作成イベントだけ抽出
npx wrangler r2 sql query "lake" "
  SELECT
    created_at,
    action,
    data['title'] AS title,
    data['identifier'] AS identifier,
    data['state'] AS state,
    actor['name'] AS actor_name
  FROM streaming.linear_events
  WHERE type = 'Issue' AND action = 'create'
  ORDER BY created_at DESC
  LIMIT 20
"

# 誰が一番 Issue を更新しているか
npx wrangler r2 sql query "lake" "
  SELECT
    actor['name'] AS actor_name,
    COUNT(*) AS update_count
  FROM streaming.linear_events
  WHERE type = 'Issue' AND action = 'update'
  GROUP BY actor['name']
  ORDER BY update_count DESC
"

# 特定の日のコメントイベント
npx wrangler r2 sql query "lake" "
  SELECT
    created_at,
    data['body'] AS comment_body,
    data['issue'] AS issue
  FROM streaming.linear_events
  WHERE type = 'Comment'
    AND created_at >= '2026-04-12T00:00:00Z'
    AND created_at < '2026-04-13T00:00:00Z'
"
```

#### DuckDB（ローカルから直接）

```sql
INSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;
INSTALL httpfs;  LOAD httpfs;

CREATE SECRET r2_secret (TYPE ICEBERG, TOKEN '<token>');
ATTACH '<warehouse_name>' AS lake (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT '<catalog_uri>');

-- 同じ SQL が使える
SELECT type, action, COUNT(*) AS cnt
FROM lake.streaming.linear_events
GROUP BY type, action
ORDER BY cnt DESC;
```

#### R2 SQL REST API

```bash
curl -X POST \
  "https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/{ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/lake" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${TOKEN}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query": "SELECT * FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 5"}'
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `data` と `actor` は `json` 型なので、R2 SQL ではブラケット記法（`data['title']`）でネストしたフィールドにアクセスできます
- Pipelines が自動付与する `__ingest_ts`（DAY パーティション）のおかげで、時間範囲のクエリは Parquet ファイルのプルーニングが効きます
- DuckDB からは Iceberg REST Catalog 経由で同じテーブルに直接アクセスでき、ローカルでの分析やダッシュボード構築に使えます
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-12-linear-webhook-pipelines-design.md",
  "content": "# Linear Webhook → Pipelines → R2 Iceberg 設計\n\n**Issue**: [TA-443](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-443) (P1: Pipelines ストリーミング取り込み — Linear Webhook 部分)\n**Date**: 2026-04-12\n**Status**: Approved\n\n## 概要\n\nLinear Webhook でリアルタイムイベント（Issue 作成/更新/コメント等）を受信し、Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog に Apache Iceberg 形式で書き込む。\n\n既存の ingestion Worker（6時間ごとの Cron スナップショット）は維持し、Webhook はイベントストリーム（CDC）として併用する。\n\n## アーキテクチャ\n\n```\nLinear Webhook (HTTP POST)\n    │\n    │  Headers: Linear-Signature, Linear-Event, Linear-Delivery\n    │\n    ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────┐\n│  streaming/ Worker (Hono)   │\n│                             │\n│  POST /webhooks/linear      │\n│  1. 署名検証 (HMAC-SHA256)  │\n│  2. ペイロード正規化         │\n│  3. env.STREAM.send()       │\n│                             │\n│  GET /health                │\n└──────────┬──────────────────┘\n           │ Pipeline binding\n           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────┐\n│  Pipelines                  │\n│                             │\n│  Stream: linear-events      │\n│    ↓                        │\n│  Pipeline: linear-events-   │\n│            pipeline         │\n│  SQL: パススルー（初期）      │\n│    ↓                        │\n│  Sink: linear-events-sink   │\n│    → R2 Data Catalog        │\n│      namespace: streaming   │\n│      table: linear_events   │\n│      format: Parquet/zstd   │\n└─────────────────────────────┘\n           │\n           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────┐\n│  R2 Bucket: lake (新規)      │\n│  APAC region                │\n│  Data Catalog 有効化         │\n│                             │\n│  Iceberg table:             │\n│  streaming.linear_events    │\n└─────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 設計方針: Thin Proxy パターン\n\nWorker は署名検証 + 最小限の正規化 + `env.STREAM.send()` のみ。変換ロジックは Pipelines の SQL に寄せる。\n\n**理由:**\n- Worker の責務が最小限で CPU 時間が短い\n- SQL 変換の変更は Pipeline の再作成のみで済む（Worker の再デプロイ不要）\n- 将来 GitHub/Stripe/Slack Webhook を追加するときもシンプルに拡張できる\n\n### 既存 ingestion との関係\n\n| 方式 | 用途 | 出力先 |\n|------|------|--------|\n| Cron スナップショット（既存） | 現在の状態のスナップショット | `data-lake` (Parquet) |\n| Webhook ストリーミング（今回） | リアルタイムのイベントログ（CDC） | `lake` (Iceberg) |\n\n将来的に ingestion も `lake` バケットに移行予定。\n\n## Stream スキーマ\n\n全 Linear Webhook リソースを1つの Structured Stream で受け取る。\n\n### スキーマ定義 (`streaming/schema/linear-events.json`)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"fields\": [\n    { \"name\": \"action\",            \"type\": \"string\",    \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"type\",              \"type\": \"string\",    \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"created_at\",        \"type\": \"timestamp\", \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"webhook_id\",        \"type\": \"string\",    \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"webhook_timestamp\", \"type\": \"int64\",     \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"organization_id\",   \"type\": \"string\",    \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"url\",               \"type\": \"string\",    \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"actor\",             \"type\": \"json\",      \"required\": false },\n    { \"name\": \"data\",              \"type\": \"json\",      \"required\": true },\n    { \"name\": \"updated_from\",      \"type\": \"json\",      \"required\": false }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### フィールド設計判断\n\n| フィールド | 型 | 理由 |\n|-----------|------|------|\n| `action` | `string` | `create`/`update`/`remove` でフィルタ。top-level でクエリ性能向上 |\n| `type` | `string` | `Issue`/`Comment`/`Project` 等でフィルタ |\n| `created_at` | `timestamp` | イベント発生時刻。時系列分析の軸 |\n| `webhook_id` | `string` | 重複排除に使用 |\n| `webhook_timestamp` | `int64` | UNIX ms。リプレイ攻撃検出にも使用 |\n| `organization_id` | `string` | マルチワークスペース対応の余地 |\n| `actor` | `json` | 誰が操作したか。構造がリソースにより異なる |\n| `data` | `json` | エンティティ本体。リソースタイプごとに構造が異なる |\n| `updated_from` | `json` | update 時のみ存在。変更前の値 |\n\n### 対応リソースタイプ\n\nIssues, Comments, Issue attachments, Issue labels, Comment reactions, Projects, Project updates, Documents, Initiatives, Initiative Updates, Cycles, Customers, Customer Requests, Users\n\n## Worker 実装設計\n\n### ディレクトリ構成\n\n```\nstreaming/\n├── src/\n│   ├── index.ts              # Hono アプリ + エントリポイント\n│   ├── types.ts              # 型定義（Bindings, Webhook ペイロード等）\n│   ├── webhooks/\n│   │   └── linear.ts         # Linear Webhook ハンドラ\n│   └── lib/\n│       └── signature.ts      # HMAC-SHA256 署名検証ユーティリティ\n├── schema/\n│   └── linear-events.json    # Stream スキーマ定義\n├── sql/\n│   └── linear-pipeline.sql   # Pipeline SQL\n├── wrangler.jsonc\n├── package.json\n├── biome.json\n└── tsconfig.json\n```\n\n### エンドポイント\n\n| メソッド | パス | 処理 |\n|---------|------|------|\n| `POST` | `/webhooks/linear` | Webhook 受信・署名検証・正規化・Stream send |\n| `GET` | `/health` | ヘルスチェック |\n\n### Webhook 受信フロー\n\n1. `Linear-Signature` ヘッダーで HMAC-SHA256 署名検証（Web Crypto API）\n2. `webhookTimestamp` が 60 秒以内か検証（リプレイ攻撃防止）\n3. ペイロードを Stream スキーマに正規化（camelCase → snake_case）\n4. `env.STREAM.send([normalizedEvent])` で Pipelines に送信\n5. 成功時 `200 OK`、署名不正時 `401`、エラー時 `500`\n\n### 署名検証\n\n```typescript\n// Web Crypto API（Workers ネイティブ）\nconst key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n  \"raw\",\n  new TextEncoder().encode(secret),\n  { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n  false,\n  [\"sign\"]\n);\nconst signature = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, rawBody);\n// hex エンコードして Linear-Signature ヘッダーと定数時間比較\n```\n\n### 正規化処理\n\nLinear ペイロードのキーを snake_case に変換：\n- `createdAt` → `created_at`\n- `webhookTimestamp` → `webhook_timestamp`\n- `webhookId` → `webhook_id`\n- `organizationId` → `organization_id`\n- `updatedFrom` → `updated_from`\n\n`data`、`actor`、`updatedFrom` の中身はそのまま `json` として格納。\n\n### Wrangler 設定 (`wrangler.jsonc`)\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"name\": \"streaming\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-04-01\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"pipelines\": [\n    {\n      \"pipeline\": \"<STREAM_ID>\",\n      \"binding\": \"STREAM\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### シークレット\n\n- `LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — `wrangler secret put` で設定\n\n## Pipelines 構成\n\n### Stream 作成\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines streams create linear-events \\\n  --schema-file streaming/schema/linear-events.json \\\n  --http-enabled false\n```\n\n- HTTP エンドポイント無効（Worker バインディングのみ）\n\n### Sink 作成\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines sinks create linear-events-sink \\\n  --type r2-data-catalog \\\n  --bucket lake \\\n  --namespace streaming \\\n  --table linear_events \\\n  --compression zstd \\\n  --roll-interval 300 \\\n  --catalog-token $R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\n```\n\n- **namespace**: `streaming`（将来 `streaming.github_events` 等を追加する命名体系）\n- **roll-interval**: 300 秒（5分）。Linear Webhook の頻度では十分\n- **compression**: zstd（デフォルト、最良の圧縮率）\n\n### Pipeline 作成\n\n```sql\n-- streaming/sql/linear-pipeline.sql\nINSERT INTO linear_events_sink\nSELECT\n  action,\n  type,\n  created_at,\n  webhook_id,\n  webhook_timestamp,\n  organization_id,\n  url,\n  actor,\n  data,\n  updated_from\nFROM linear_events\n```\n\n```bash\nnpx wrangler pipelines create linear-events-pipeline \\\n  --sql-file streaming/sql/linear-pipeline.sql\n```\n\n初期はパススルー。将来的に追加可能な変換：\n- `WHERE action != 'remove'` でソフトデリートフィルタ\n- PII マスキング\n- タイムスタンプ正規化\n\n## インフラ（Pulumi）\n\n### R2 バケット `lake` 追加\n\n`infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go` に追加：\n\n```go\nlakeR2Bucket, err := r2.NewBucket(ctx, \"lake\", &r2.BucketArgs{\n    AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),\n    Name:      pulumi.String(\"lake\"),\n    Location:  pulumi.String(\"APAC\"),\n}, pulumi.Protect(true))\n```\n\n- APAC リージョン、`Protect: true`\n- Data Catalog の有効化: `npx wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable lake`（Pulumi 未サポート）\n\n### Pipelines リソース\n\nWrangler CLI で手動作成（Pulumi の Cloudflare プロバイダに Pipelines リソースが未実装）。\n\n作成後の Stream ID を `wrangler.jsonc` に設定。\n\n## デプロイ\n\n### streaming Worker\n\nCloudflare Dashboard の GitHub 連携で自動デプロイ：\n- `main` → 本番デプロイ\n- PR → プレビュー URL\n\n### Linear Webhook 設定\n\nLinear Settings > Administration > API > Webhooks:\n- **URL**: `https://streaming.<subdomain>.workers.dev/webhooks/linear`\n- **Resource types**: All\n- **Signing secret**: コピーして `wrangler secret put LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET`\n\n## クエリ例\n\n### R2 SQL（Wrangler CLI）\n\n```bash\n# Issue の作成イベント\nnpx wrangler r2 sql query \"lake\" \"\n  SELECT\n    created_at,\n    action,\n    data['title'] AS title,\n    data['identifier'] AS identifier,\n    actor['name'] AS actor_name\n  FROM streaming.linear_events\n  WHERE type = 'Issue' AND action = 'create'\n  ORDER BY created_at DESC\n  LIMIT 20\n\"\n\n# アクター別 Issue 更新回数\nnpx wrangler r2 sql query \"lake\" \"\n  SELECT\n    actor['name'] AS actor_name,\n    COUNT(*) AS update_count\n  FROM streaming.linear_events\n  WHERE type = 'Issue' AND action = 'update'\n  GROUP BY actor['name']\n  ORDER BY update_count DESC\n\"\n```\n\n### DuckDB\n\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;\nINSTALL httpfs;  LOAD httpfs;\nCREATE SECRET r2_secret (TYPE ICEBERG, TOKEN '<token>');\nATTACH '<warehouse_name>' AS lake (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT '<catalog_uri>');\n\nSELECT type, action, COUNT(*) AS cnt\nFROM lake.streaming.linear_events\nGROUP BY type, action\nORDER BY cnt DESC;\n```\n\n## テスト戦略\n\n1. **署名検証テスト**: 正しい署名 → 200、不正な署名 → 401、期限切れタイムスタンプ → 401\n2. **正規化テスト**: Linear ペイロード → Stream スキーマへの変換が正しいか\n3. **E2E テスト**: Linear Webhook をトリガーし、R2 SQL でデータを確認\n"
}
```

1	import { Hono } from "hono";
2	import github, { runSync as runGitHubSync } from "./services/github";
3	import linear, { runSync as runLinearSync } from "./services/linear";
4	import oura, { runSync as runOuraSync } from "./services/oura";
5	import withings, { runSync as runWithingsSync } from "./services/withings";
6	import type { Env, SyncResult } from "./types";
7	
8	const app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();
9	
10	// Service routes
11	app.route("/github", github);
12	app.route("/oura", oura);
13	app.route("/linear", linear);
14	app.route("/withings", withings);
15	
16	// Root
17	app.get("/", (c) => {
18	  return c.json({
19	    name: "ingestion",
20	    services: ["github", "oura", "linear", "withings"],
21	    endpoints: {
22	      "POST /sync": "Sync all services",
23	      "GET /health": "Health check",
24	      github: {
25	        "POST /github/sync": "Sync GitHub data to R2 Parquet",
26	        "GET /github/stats": "R2 file counts",
27	      },
28	      oura: {
29	        "GET /oura/auth": "Start OAuth2 authorization",
30	        "GET /oura/callback": "OAuth2 callback",
31	        "POST /oura/sync": "Sync Oura data to R2 Parquet",
32	        "GET /oura/stats": "R2 file counts",
33	      },
34	      linear: {
35	        "POST /linear/sync": "Sync Linear data to R2 Parquet",
36	        "GET /linear/stats": "R2 file info",
37	      },
38	      withings: {
39	        "GET /withings/auth": "Start OAuth2 authorization",
40	        "GET /withings/callback": "OAuth2 callback",
41	        "POST /withings/sync": "Sync Withings data to R2 Parquet (?start_date&end_date=YYYY-MM-DD)",
42	        "GET /withings/stats": "R2 file counts",
43	      },
44	    },
45	  });
46	});
47	
48	// Sync all services
49	app.post("/sync", async (c) => {
50	  const results: SyncResult[] = [];
51	
52	  try {
53	    results.push(await runGitHubSync(c.env));
54	  } catch (e) {
55	    results.push({
56	      service: "github",
57	      success: false,
58	      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
59	    });
60	  }
61	
62	  try {
63	    results.push(await runOuraSync(c.env));
64	  } catch (e) {
65	    results.push({
66	      service: "oura",
67	      success: false,
68	      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
69	    });
70	  }
71	
72	  try {
73	    results.push(await runLinearSync(c.env));
74	  } catch (e) {
75	    results.push({
76	      service: "linear",
77	      success: false,
78	      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
79	    });
80	  }
81	
82	  try {
83	    results.push(await runWithingsSync(c.env));
84	  } catch (e) {
85	    results.push({
86	      service: "withings",
87	      success: false,
88	      message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
89	    });
90	  }
91	
92	  return c.json({
93	    results,
94	    success: results.every((r) => r.success),
95	  });
96	});
97	
98	// Health check
99	app.get("/health", (c) => c.json({ status: "ok" }));
100	
101	// Scheduled handler (Cron)
102	// "0 0 * * *"    → GitHub (1日1回 UTC 0:00 / JST 9:00)
103	// "0 */12 * * *" → Oura   (12時間毎 UTC 0:00,12:00 / JST 9:00,21:00)
104	// "0 */6 * * *"  → Linear (6時間毎 UTC 0:00,6:00,12:00,18:00)
105	const scheduled: ExportedHandlerScheduledHandler<Env> = async (event, env) => {
106	  console.log("Scheduled sync started:", event.cron);
107	
108	  if (event.cron === "0 0 * * *") {
109	    try {
110	      const result = await runGitHubSync(env);
111	      console.log("GitHub sync completed:", result);
112	    } catch (e) {
113	      console.error("GitHub sync failed:", e);
114	    }
115	  }
116	
117	  if (event.cron === "0 */12 * * *") {
118	    try {
119	      const result = await runOuraSync(env);
120	      console.log("Oura sync completed:", result);
121	    } catch (e) {
122	      console.error("Oura sync failed:", e);
123	    }
124	
125	    try {
126	      const result = await runWithingsSync(env);
127	      console.log("Withings sync completed:", result);
128	    } catch (e) {
129	      console.error("Withings sync failed:", e);
130	    }
131	  }
132	
133	  if (event.cron === "0 */6 * * *") {
134	    try {
135	      const result = await runLinearSync(env);
136	      console.log("Linear sync completed:", result);
137	    } catch (e) {
138	      console.error("Linear sync failed:", e);
139	    }
140	  }
141	};
142	
143	export default {
144	  fetch: app.fetch,
145	  scheduled,
146	};
147

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"github.com/pulumi/pulumi-cloudflare/sdk/v6/go/cloudflare"
5		"github.com/pulumi/pulumi/sdk/v3/go/pulumi"
6	)
7	
8	type CloudflareOutputs struct {
9		D1DatabaseId   pulumi.IDOutput
10		D1DatabaseName pulumi.StringOutput
11		R2BucketName   pulumi.StringOutput
12		KvNamespaceId  pulumi.IDOutput
13	}
14	
15	func createCloudflareResources(ctx *pulumi.Context, accountId string) (*CloudflareOutputs, error) {
16		// ===========================================
17		// D1 Database
18		// ===========================================
19		// 既存のD1データベースをインポートして管理
20		// pulumi import cloudflare:index/d1Database:D1Database raw <account_id>/<database_id>
21		rawDb, err := cloudflare.NewD1Database(ctx, "raw", &cloudflare.D1DatabaseArgs{
22			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
23			Name:      pulumi.String("raw"),
24		}, pulumi.Protect(true))
25		if err != nil {
26			return nil, err
27		}
28	
29		// ===========================================
30		// R2 Bucket (for data lake)
31		// ===========================================
32		dataLake, err := cloudflare.NewR2Bucket(ctx, "data-lake", &cloudflare.R2BucketArgs{
33			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
34			Name:      pulumi.String("data-lake"),
35			Location:  pulumi.String("APAC"),
36		})
37		if err != nil {
38			return nil, err
39		}
40	
41		// ===========================================
42		// Workers KV Namespace (for caching)
43		// ===========================================
44		cacheKv, err := cloudflare.NewWorkersKvNamespace(ctx, "data-cache", &cloudflare.WorkersKvNamespaceArgs{
45			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
46			Title:     pulumi.String("data-cache"),
47		})
48		if err != nil {
49			return nil, err
50		}
51	
52		// ===========================================
53		// AI Gateway (for data-agent)
54		// ===========================================
55		// AI Gateway is not yet supported by the Pulumi Cloudflare provider.
56		// Manage via Cloudflare Dashboard or API:
57		//   wrangler ai-gateway create data-agent-gateway
58		// Gateway config: cache TTL 3600s, rate limit 100 req/60s, logs disabled (health data)
59	
60		return &CloudflareOutputs{
61			D1DatabaseId:   rawDb.ID(),
62			D1DatabaseName: rawDb.Name,
63			R2BucketName:   dataLake.Name,
64			KvNamespaceId:  cacheKv.ID(),
65		}, nil
66	}
67

5	create table if not exists github_users (
6	    id integer primary key,
7	    login text unique not null,
8	    name text,
9	    avatar_url text,
10	    synced_at text not null
11	);
12	
13	create table if not exists github_repos (
14	    id integer primary key,
15	    owner_id integer not null,
16	    name text not null,
17	    full_name text unique not null,
18	    description text,
19	    language text,
20	    stars integer default 0,
21	    forks integer default 0,
22	    is_private integer default 0,
23	    default_branch text,
24	    synced_at text not null,
25	    foreign key (owner_id) references github_users(id)
26	);
27	
28	create table if not exists github_commits (
29	    sha text primary key,
30	    repo_id integer not null,
31	    message text,
32	    author_name text,
33	    author_email text,
34	    author_date text not null,
35	    additions integer default 0,
36	    deletions integer default 0,
37	    files_changed integer default 0,
38	    synced_at text not null,
39	    foreign key (repo_id) references github_repos(id)
40	);
41	
42	create index if not exists idx_github_commits_repo on github_commits(repo_id);
43	create index if not exists idx_github_commits_date on github_commits(author_date desc);
44	create index if not exists idx_github_repos_owner on github_repos(owner_id);
45	create index if not exists idx_github_repos_language on github_repos(language);
46	
47	create view if not exists v_daily_commits as
48	select
49	    date(author_date) as commit_date,
50	    count(*) as commit_count,
51	    sum(additions) as total_additions,
52	    sum(deletions) as total_deletions,
53	    count(distinct repo_id) as repos_touched
54	from github_commits
55	group by date(author_date)
56	order by commit_date desc;
57	
58	create view if not exists v_repo_stats as
59	select
60	    r.full_name,
61	    r.language,
62	    count(c.sha) as total_commits,
63	    sum(c.additions) as total_additions,
64	    sum(c.deletions) as total_deletions,
65	    max(c.author_date) as last_commit_date
66	from github_repos r
67	left join github_commits c on r.id = c.repo_id
68	group by r.id
69	order by total_commits desc;
70	
71	create view if not exists v_weekly_activity as
72	select
73	    strftime('%Y-W%W', author_date) as week,
74	    count(*) as commits,
75	    count(distinct repo_id) as active_repos,
76	    sum(additions + deletions) as lines_changed
77	from github_commits
78	group by strftime('%Y-W%W', author_date)
79	order by week desc;
80	
81	create table if not exists oauth_tokens (

85	    token_type text default 'Bearer',

106

111	    active_calories integer,

  "content": "# Linear Webhook → Pipelines → R2 Iceberg 実装プラン\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Linear Webhook をリアルタイムで受信し、Cloudflare Pipelines 経由で R2 Data Catalog に Apache Iceberg テーブルとして書き込む streaming Worker を構築する。\n\n**Architecture:** 新規 `streaming/` Worker（Hono）が Linear Webhook を受信し、HMAC-SHA256 署名検証後、ペイロードを正規化して `env.STREAM.send()` で Pipelines に送信する Thin Proxy パターン。Pipelines（Stream → Pipeline → Sink）が R2 バケット `lake` に Iceberg 形式で書き込む。\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Hono, Cloudflare Workers, Cloudflare Pipelines, R2 Data Catalog, Vitest + @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers, Biome, Pulumi (Go)\n\n**Design Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-12-linear-webhook-pipelines-design.md`\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n### 新規作成\n\n| ファイル | 責務 |\n|---------|------|\n| `streaming/src/index.ts` | Hono アプリ + エントリポイント |\n| `streaming/src/types.ts` | 型定義（Env, LinearWebhookPayload 等） |\n| `streaming/src/webhooks/linear.ts` | Linear Webhook ハンドラ（正規化 + send） |\n| `streaming/src/lib/signature.ts` | HMAC-SHA256 署名検証ユーティリティ |\n| `streaming/src/__tests__/env.d.ts` | テスト用の環境型定義 |\n| `streaming/src/__tests__/signature.test.ts` | 署名検証のテスト |\n| `streaming/src/__tests__/linear.test.ts` | Linear Webhook ハンドラのテスト |\n| `streaming/src/__tests__/index.test.ts` | エンドポイント統合テスト |\n| `streaming/schema/linear-events.json` | Stream スキーマ定義 |\n| `streaming/sql/linear-pipeline.sql` | Pipeline SQL |\n| `streaming/wrangler.jsonc` | Wrangler 設定 |\n| `streaming/package.json` | 依存関係 |\n| `streaming/tsconfig.json` | TypeScript 設定 |\n| `streaming/biome.json` | Biome 設定 |\n| `streaming/vitest.config.ts` | Vitest 設定 |\n\n### 変更\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|---------|---------|\n| `infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go` | R2 バケット `lake` の追加 + Outputs 更新 |\n| `infrastructure/pulumi/main.go` | `lake` の Export 追加 |\n\n---\n\n## Task 1: プロジェクトスキャフォールド\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `streaming/package.json`\n- Create: `streaming/tsconfig.json`\n- Create: `streaming/biome.json`\n- Create: `streaming/wrangler.jsonc`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: `streaming/package.json` を作成**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"streaming\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"wrangler dev\",\n    \"deploy\": \"wrangler deploy --minify\",\n    \"test\": \"vitest\",\n    \"test:run\": \"vitest run\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\",\n    \"cf-typegen\": \"wrangler types --env-interface CloudflareBindings\",\n    \"lint\": \"biome lint .\",\n    \"lint:fix\": \"biome lint --write .\",\n    \"format\": \"biome format --write .\",\n    \"check\": \"biome check .\",\n    \"check:fix\": \"biome check --write .\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"hono\": \"^4.11.7\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@biomejs/biome\": \"^2.3.13\",\n    \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\": \"^0.8.0\",\n    \"@cloudflare/workers-types\": \"^4.20250124.0\",\n    \"typescript\": \"^5.7.0\",\n    \"vitest\": \"^3.0.0\",\n    \"wrangler\": \"^4.4.0\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: `streaming/tsconfig.json` を作成**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"compilerOptions\": {\n    \"target\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"module\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"moduleResolution\": \"Bundler\",\n    \"strict\": true,\n    \"skipLibCheck\": true,\n    \"lib\": [\"ESNext\"],\n    \"jsx\": \"react-jsx\",\n    \"jsxImportSource\": \"hono/jsx\",\n    \"types\": [\"@cloudflare/workers-types/experimental\", \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\"]\n  },\n  \"include\": [\"src/**/*.ts\"],\n  \"exclude\": [\"node_modules\"]\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: `streaming/biome.json` を作成**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"https://biomejs.dev/schemas/2.3.13/schema.json\",\n  \"vcs\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"clientKind\": \"git\",\n    \"useIgnoreFile\": true\n  },\n  \"files\": {\n    \"ignoreUnknown\": true,\n    \"includes\": [\"**\", \"!**/node_modules\", \"!**/dist\", \"!**/.wrangler\", \"!**/wrangler-env.d.ts\"]\n  },\n  \"formatter\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"indentStyle\": \"space\",\n    \"indentWidth\": 2,\n    \"lineWidth\": 100\n  },\n  \"linter\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"rules\": {\n      \"recommended\": true,\n      \"suspicious\": {\n        \"noExplicitAny\": \"warn\"\n      },\n      \"complexity\": {\n        \"noForEach\": \"off\"\n      }\n    }\n  },\n  \"javascript\": {\n    \"formatter\": {\n      \"quoteStyle\": \"double\",\n      \"semicolons\": \"always\",\n      \"trailingCommas\": \"es5\"\n    }\n  },\n  \"assist\": { \"actions\": { \"source\": { \"organizeImports\": \"on\" } } }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: `streaming/wrangler.jsonc` を作成**\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json\",\n  \"name\": \"streaming\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-04-01\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n\n  \"preview_urls\": true,\n  \"observability\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"head_sampling_rate\": 1\n  },\n  \"pipelines\": [\n    {\n      \"pipeline\": \"REPLACE_WITH_STREAM_ID\",\n      \"binding\": \"STREAM\"\n    }\n  ]\n  // シークレット:\n  //   wrangler secret put LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: 依存関係をインストール**\n\nRun: `cd streaming && pnpm install`\nExpected: `node_modules/` が作成され、`pnpm-lock.yaml` が生成される\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add streaming/package.json streaming/pnpm-lock.yaml streaming/tsconfig.json streaming/biome.json streaming/wrangler.jsonc\ngit commit -m \"chore: scaffold streaming worker project\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 2: 型定義\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `streaming/src/types.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: `streaming/src/types.ts` を作成**\n\n```typescript\nimport type { Pipeline } from \"cloudflare:pipelines\";\n\nexport interface Env {\n  STREAM: Pipeline;\n  LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET: string;\n}\n\n// Linear Webhook の生ペイロード（camelCase）\nexport interface LinearWebhookPayload {\n  action: \"create\" | \"update\" | \"remove\";\n  type: string;\n  createdAt: string;\n  webhookId: string;\n  webhookTimestamp: number;\n  organizationId: string;\n  url?: string;\n  actor?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  data: Record<string, unknown>;\n  updatedFrom?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n// Stream に送信する正規化済みイベント（snake_case）\nexport interface LinearStreamEvent {\n  action: string;\n  type: string;\n  created_at: string;\n  webhook_id: string;\n  webhook_timestamp: number;\n  organization_id: string;\n  url: string | null;\n  actor: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n  data: Record<string, unknown>;\n  updated_from: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add streaming/src/types.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: add type definitions for streaming worker\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 3: 署名検証ユーティリティ（TDD）\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `streaming/src/lib/signature.ts`\n- Test: `streaming/src/__tests__/signature.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: テスト用の型定義ファイルを作成**\n\nCreate `streaming/src/__tests__/env.d.ts`:\n\n```typescript\ndeclare module \"cloudflare:test\" {\n  interface ProvidedEnv {\n    STREAM: import(\"cloudflare:pipelines\").Pipeline;\n    LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET: string;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: vitest.config.ts を作成**\n\nCreate `streaming/vitest.config.ts`:\n\n```typescript\nimport { defineWorkersConfig } from \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers/config\";\n\nexport default defineWorkersConfig({\n  test: {\n    poolOptions: {\n      workers: {\n        wrangler: { configPath: \"./wrangler.jsonc\" },\n      },\n    },\n  },\n});\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: 失敗するテストを書く**\n\nCreate `streaming/src/__tests__/signature.test.ts`:\n\n```typescript\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { timingSafeEqual, verifySignature } from \"../lib/signature\";\n\ndescribe(\"timingSafeEqual\", () => {\n  it(\"returns true for equal strings\", () => {\n    expect(timingSafeEqual(\"abc\", \"abc\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for different strings\", () => {\n    expect(timingSafeEqual(\"abc\", \"def\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for different lengths\", () => {\n    expect(timingSafeEqual(\"abc\", \"abcd\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"verifySignature\", () => {\n  const secret = \"test-webhook-secret\";\n\n  it(\"returns true for valid signature\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    // Pre-computed HMAC-SHA256 of the body with the secret\n    const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n      \"raw\",\n      encoder.encode(secret),\n      { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n      false,\n      [\"sign\"]\n    );\n    const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n    const hex = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n      .join(\"\");\n\n    const result = await verifySignature(body, hex, secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for invalid signature\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const result = await verifySignature(body, \"invalid-signature\", secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for tampered body\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const tamperedBody = '{\"action\":\"remove\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n      \"raw\",\n      encoder.encode(secret),\n      { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n      false,\n      [\"sign\"]\n    );\n    const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n    const hex = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n      .join(\"\");\n\n    const result = await verifySignature(tamperedBody, hex, secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: テストが失敗することを確認**\n\nRun: `cd streaming && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: FAIL — `Cannot find module '../lib/signature'`\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: 最小限の実装を書く**\n\nCreate `streaming/src/lib/signature.ts`:\n\n```typescript\n/**\n * 定数時間での文字列比較（タイミング攻撃防止）\n */\nexport function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {\n  if (a.length !== b.length) {\n    return false;\n  }\n  let result = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {\n    result |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);\n  }\n  return result === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Linear Webhook の HMAC-SHA256 署名を検証する\n *\n * @param body - リクエストの生ボディ文字列\n * @param signature - Linear-Signature ヘッダーの値（hex エンコード）\n * @param secret - Webhook signing secret\n * @returns 署名が有効なら true\n */\nexport async function verifySignature(\n  body: string,\n  signature: string,\n  secret: string\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n    \"raw\",\n    encoder.encode(secret),\n    { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    false,\n    [\"sign\"]\n  );\n  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n  const computed = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n    .join(\"\");\n\n  return timingSafeEqual(computed, signature);\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: テストがパスすることを確認**\n\nRun: `cd streaming && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: All 6 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add streaming/src/lib/signature.ts streaming/src/__tests__/signature.test.ts streaming/src/__tests__/env.d.ts streaming/vitest.config.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: add HMAC-SHA256 signature verification for webhook\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 4: Linear Webhook ハンドラ（TDD）\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `streaming/src/webhooks/linear.ts`\n- Test: `streaming/src/__tests__/linear.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: 失敗するテストを書く**\n\nCreate `streaming/src/__tests__/linear.test.ts`:\n\n```typescript\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { normalizePayload, validateTimestamp } from \"../webhooks/linear\";\nimport type { LinearWebhookPayload } from \"../types\";\n\ndescribe(\"normalizePayload\", () => {\n  it(\"converts camelCase keys to snake_case\", () => {\n    const payload: LinearWebhookPayload = {\n      action: \"create\",\n      type: \"Issue\",\n      createdAt: \"2026-04-12T10:00:00.000Z\",\n      webhookId: \"wh-123\",\n      webhookTimestamp: 1744444800000,\n      organizationId: \"org-456\",\n      url: \"https://linear.app/team/issue/ID-1\",\n      actor: { id: \"user-1\", name: \"Test User\" },\n      data: { id: \"issue-1\", title: \"Test Issue\" },\n      updatedFrom: { title: \"Old Title\" },\n    };\n\n    const result = normalizePayload(payload);\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({\n      action: \"create\",\n      type: \"Issue\",\n      created_at: \"2026-04-12T10:00:00.000Z\",\n      webhook_id: \"wh-123\",\n      webhook_timestamp: 1744444800000,\n      organization_id: \"org-456\",\n      url: \"https://linear.app/team/issue/ID-1\",\n      actor: { id: \"user-1\", name: \"Test User\" },\n      data: { id: \"issue-1\", title: \"Test Issue\" },\n      updated_from: { title: \"Old Title\" },\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"sets optional fields to null when absent\", () => {\n    const payload: LinearWebhookPayload = {\n      action: \"create\",\n      type: \"Issue\",\n      createdAt: \"2026-04-12T10:00:00.000Z\",\n      webhookId: \"wh-123\",\n      webhookTimestamp: 1744444800000,\n      organizationId: \"org-456\",\n      data: { id: \"issue-1\" },\n    };\n\n    const result = normalizePayload(payload);\n\n    expect(result.url).toBeNull();\n    expect(result.actor).toBeNull();\n    expect(result.updated_from).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"validateTimestamp\", () => {\n  it(\"returns true for timestamp within 60 seconds\", () => {\n    const now = Date.now();\n    expect(validateTimestamp(now)).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns true for timestamp 30 seconds ago\", () => {\n    const thirtySecondsAgo = Date.now() - 30_000;\n    expect(validateTimestamp(thirtySecondsAgo)).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for timestamp older than 60 seconds\", () => {\n    const twoMinutesAgo = Date.now() - 120_000;\n    expect(validateTimestamp(twoMinutesAgo)).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for timestamp in the far future\", () => {\n    const \"hono\";\nimport { verifySignature } from \"../lib/signature\";\nimport type { Env, LinearStreamEvent, LinearWebhookPayload } from \"../types\";\n\nconst TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS = 60_000;\n\n/**\n * Linear Webhook ペイロードを Stream スキーマに正規化する\n */\nexport function normalizePayload(payload: LinearWebhookPayload): LinearStreamEvent {\n  return {\n    action: payload.action,\n    type: payload.type,\n    created_at: payload.createdAt,\n    webhook_id: payload.webhookId,\n    webhook_timestamp: payload.webhookTimestamp,\n    organization_id: payload.organizationId,\n    url: payload.url ?? null,\n    actor: payload.actor ?? null,\n    data: payload.data,\n    updated_from: payload.updatedFrom ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Webhook タイムスタンプが許容範囲内か検証する（リプレイ攻撃防止）\n */\nexport function validateTimestamp(webhookTimestamp: number): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const diff = Math.abs(now - webhookTimestamp);\n  return diff <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS;\n}\n\nconst linear = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();\n\nlinear.post(\"/\", async (c) => {\n  const signature = c.req.header(\"Linear-Signature\");\n  if (!signature) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing signature\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const rawBody = await c.req.text();\n\n  const isValid = await verifySignature(rawBody, signature, c.env.LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET);\n  if (!isValid) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid signature\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const payload: LinearWebhookPayload = JSON.parse(rawBody);\n\n  if (!validateTimestamp(payload.webhookTimestamp)) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Timestamp out of range\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const event = normalizePayload(payload);\n\n  await c.env.STREAM.send([event]);\n\n  return c.json({ success: true }, 200);\n});\n\nexport default linear;\n```\n\n- [ ]   expect(json).toEqual({ status: \"ok\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns service info on GET /\", async () => {\n    const res = await SELF.fetch(\"https://example.com/\");\n    expect(res.status).toBe(200);\n\n    const json = await res.json();\n    expect(json).toHaveProperty(\"name\", \"streaming\");\n    expect(json).toHaveProperty(\"endpoints\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns 404 for unknown routes\", async () => {\n    const res = await SELF.fetch(\"https://example.com/unknown\");\n    expect(res.status).toBe(404);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns 401 when Linear webhook has no body: JSON.stringify({ action: \"create\", type: \"Issue\" }),\n    });\n    expect(res.status).toBe(401);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: テストが失敗することを確認**\n\nRun: `cd streaming && pnpm \"./types\";\nimport linear from \"./webhooks/linear\";\n\nconst app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();\n\n// Webhook routes\napp.route(\"/webhooks/linear\", linear);\n\n// Root\napp.get(\"/\", (c) => {\n  return c.json({\n    name: \"streaming\",\n    endpoints: {\n      \"POST /webhooks/linear\": \"Receive Linear webhook events\",\n      \"GET /health\": \"Health check\",\n    },\n 1	# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
2	
3	Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
4	
5	```
6	Task tool (general-purpose):
7	  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
8	  prompt: |
9	    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
10	
11	    ## Task Description
12	
13	    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
14	
15	    ## Context
16	
17	    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
18	
19	    ## Before You Begin
20	
21	    If you have questions about:
22	    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
23	    - The approach or implementation strategy
24	    - Dependencies or assumptions
25	    - Anything unclear in the task description
26	
27	    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
28	
29	    ## Your Job
30	
31	    Once you're clear on requirements:
32	    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
33	    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
34	    3. Verify implementation works
35	    4. Commit your work
36	    5. Self-review (see below)
37	    6. Report back
38	
39	    Work from: [directory]
40	
41	    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
42	    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
43	
44	    ## Code Organization
45	
46	    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
47	    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
48	    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
49	    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
50	    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
51	      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
52	    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
53	      and note it as a concern in your report
54	    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
55	      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
56	
57	    ## When You're in Over Your Head
58	
59	    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
60	    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
61	
62	    **STOP and escalate when:**
63	    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
64	    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
65	    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
66	    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
67	    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
68	
69	    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
70	    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
71	    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
72	    or break the task into smaller pieces.
73	
74	    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
75	
76	    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
77	
78	    **Completeness:**
79	    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
80	    - Did I miss any requirements?
81	    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
82	
83	    **Quality:**
84	    - Is this my best work?
85	    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
86	    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
87	
88	    **Discipline:**
89	    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
90	    - Did I only build what was requested?
91	    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
92	
93	    **Testing:**
94	    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
95	    - Did I follow TDD if required?
96	    - Are tests comprehensive?
97	
98	    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
99	
100	    ## Report Format
101	
102	    When done, report:
103	    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
104	    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
105	    - What you tested and test results
106	    - Files changed
107	    - Self-review findings (if any)
108	    - Any issues or concerns
109	
110	    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
111	    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
112	    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.
113	```

22	- Are units decomposed so they can be understood and tested independently?

24	- Did this implementation create new files that are already large, or significantly grow existing files? (Don't flag pre-existing file sizes — focus on what this change contributed.)

**Modification #3: Update the return statement in cloudflare.go**

In `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go`, change the return statement (lines 73-79) from:

```go
return &CloudflareOutputs{
    D1DatabaseId:   rawDb.ID(),

    R2BucketName:   dataLake.Name,
    KvNamespaceId:  cacheKv.ID(),
}, nil
```

To:

```go
return &CloudflareOutputs{
    D1DatabaseId:     rawDb.ID(),
    D1DatabaseName:   rawDb.Name,
    R2BucketName:     dataLake.Name,
    LakeR2BucketName: lake.Name,
    KvNamespaceId:    cacheKv.ID(),
}, nil
```

**Modification #4: Add export in main.go**

In `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/main.go`, after line 28 (`ctx.Export("r2BucketName", cf.R2BucketName)`), add this new line:

```go
ctx.Export("lakeR2BucketName", cf.LakeR2BucketName)
```

So the export section (lines 26-29) becomes:

```go
ctx.Export("d1DatabaseId", cf.D1DatabaseId)
ctx.Export("d1DatabaseName", cf.D1DatabaseName)
ctx.Export("r2BucketName", cf.R2BucketName)
ctx.Export("lakeR2BucketName", cf.LakeR2BucketName)
ctx.Export("kvNamespaceId", cf.KvNamespaceId)
```

**Verification**

Run the following to verify the Go code compiles:

```bash
cd /Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi && go build ./...
```

**Commit**

```bash
git add infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go infrastructure/pulumi/main.go
git commit -m "feat: add R2 bucket 'lake' for Iceberg data lake"
```

3. Returning the lake bucket name in the outputs struct
4. Exporting the lake bucket name for use in deployments

16	func createCloudflareResources(ctx *pulumi.Context, accountId string) (*CloudflareOutputs, error) {
17		// ===========================================
18		// D1 Database
19		// ===========================================
20		// 既存のD1データベースをインポートして管理
21		// pulumi import cloudflare:index/d1Database:D1Database raw <account_id>/<database_id>
22		rawDb, err := cloudflare.NewD1Database(ctx, "raw", &cloudflare.D1DatabaseArgs{
23			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
24			Name:      pulumi.String("raw"),
25		}, pulumi.Protect(true))
26		if err != nil {
27			return nil, err
28		}
29	
30		// ===========================================
31		// R2 Bucket (for data lake)
32		// ===========================================
33		dataLake, err := cloudflare.NewR2Bucket(ctx, "data-lake", &cloudflare.R2BucketArgs{
34			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
35			Name:      pulumi.String("data-lake"),
36			Location:  pulumi.String("APAC"),
37		})
38		if err != nil {
39			return nil, err
40		}
41	
42		// ===========================================
43		// R2 Bucket (for Iceberg data lake)
44		// ===========================================
45		lake, err := cloudflare.NewR2Bucket(ctx, "lake", &cloudflare.R2BucketArgs{
46			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
47			Name:      pulumi.String("lake"),
48			Location:  pulumi.String("APAC"),
49		}, pulumi.Protect(true))
50		if err != nil {
51			return nil, err
52		}
53	
54		// ===========================================
55		// Workers KV Namespace (for caching)
56		// ===========================================
57		cacheKv, err := cloudflare.NewWorkersKvNamespace(ctx, "data-cache", &cloudflare.WorkersKvNamespaceArgs{
58			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
59			Title:     pulumi.String("data-cache"),
60		})
61		if err != nil {
62			return nil, err
63		}
64	
65		// ===========================================
66		// AI Gateway (for data-agent)
67		// ===========================================
68		// AI Gateway is not yet supported by the Pulumi Cloudflare provider.
69		// Manage via Cloudflare Dashboard or API:
70		//   wrangler ai-gateway create data-agent-gateway
71		// Gateway config: cache TTL 3600s, rate limit 100 req/60s, logs disabled (health data)
72	
73		return &CloudflareOutputs{
74			D1DatabaseId:   rawDb.ID(),
75			D1DatabaseName: rawDb.Name,
76			R2BucketName:   dataLake.Name,
77			KvNamespaceId:  cacheKv.ID(),
78		}, nil
79	}

\"def\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for different lengths\", () => {\n    expect(timingSafeEqual(\"abc\", \"abcd\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"verifySignature\", () => {\n  const secret = \"test-webhook-secret\";\n\n  it(\"returns true for valid signature\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n      \"raw\",\n      encoder.encode(secret),\n      { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n      false,\n      [\"sign\"]\n    );\n    const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n    const hex = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n      .join(\"\");\n\n    const result = await verifySignature(body, hex, secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for invalid signature\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const result = await verifySignature(body, \"invalid-signature\", secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns false for tampered body\", async () => {\n    const body = '{\"action\":\"create\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const tamperedBody = '{\"action\":\"remove\",\"type\":\"Issue\"}';\n    const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n      \"raw\",\n      encoder.encode(secret),\n      { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n      false,\n      [\"sign\"]\n    );\n    const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n    const hex = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n      .join(\"\");\n\n    const result = await verifySignature(tamperedBody, hex, secret);\n    expect(result).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/streaming/src/lib/signature.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * 定数時間での文字列比較（タイミング攻撃防止）\n */\nexport function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {\n  if (a.length !== b.length) {\n    return false;\n  }\n  let result = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {\n    result |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);\n  }\n  return result === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Linear Webhook の HMAC-SHA256 署名を検証する\n */\nexport async function verifySignature(\n  body: string,\n  signature: string,\n  secret: string\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n    \"raw\",\n    encoder.encode(secret),\n    { name: \"HMAC\", hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    false,\n    [\"sign\"]\n  );\n  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"HMAC\", key, encoder.encode(body));\n  const computed = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))\n    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n    .join(\"\");\n\n  return timingSafeEqual(computed, signature);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/streaming/src/webhooks/linear.ts",
  "content": "import { Hono } from \"hono\";\nimport { verifySignature } from \"../lib/signature\";\nimport type { Env, LinearStreamEvent, LinearWebhookPayload } from \"../types\";\n\nconst TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS = 60_000;\n\n/**\n * Linear Webhook ペイロードを Stream スキーマに正規化する\n */\nexport function normalizePayload(payload: LinearWebhookPayload): LinearStreamEvent {\n  return {\n    action: payload.action,\n    type: payload.type,\n    created_at: payload.createdAt,\n    webhook_id: payload.webhookId,\n    webhook_timestamp: payload.webhookTimestamp,\n    organization_id: payload.organizationId,\n    url: payload.url ?? null,\n    actor: payload.actor ?? null,\n    data: payload.data,\n    updated_from: payload.updatedFrom ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Webhook タイムスタンプが許容範囲内か検証する（リプレイ攻撃防止）\n */\nexport function validateTimestamp(webhookTimestamp: number): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const diff = Math.abs(now - webhookTimestamp);\n  return diff <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS;\n}\n\nconst linear = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();\n\nlinear.post(\"/\", async (c) => {\n  const signature = c.req.header(\"Linear-Signature\");\n  if (!signature) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing signature\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const rawBody = await c.req.text();\n\n  const isValid = await verifySignature(rawBody, signature, c.env.LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET);\n  if (!isValid) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid signature\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const payload: LinearWebhookPayload = JSON.parse(rawBody);\n\n  if (!validateTimestamp(payload.webhookTimestamp)) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Timestamp out of range\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const event = normalizePayload(payload);\n\n  await c.env.STREAM.send([event]);\n\n  return c.json({ success: true }, 200);\n});\n\nexport default linear;\n"
}

> AGENT

`--catalog-token` が必要です。R2 Data Catalog 用の API トークンを作成する必要があります。

**ここから先は手動操作が必要です。** Sink 作成には R2 Data Catalog の API トークンが必要で、Cloudflare Dashboard で作成する必要があります。

以下の手順を実行してください：

### 残りの手動手順

**1. API トークン作成**

Cloudflare Dashboard → My Profile → API Tokens → Create Token:
- Permission: **R2 Admin Read & Write**（R2 Data Catalog アクセスに必要）
- トークンをコピー

**2. Sink 作成**

```bash
cd streaming
npx wrangler pipelines sinks create linear_events_sink \
  --type r2-data-catalog \
  --bucket lake \
  --namespace streaming \
  --table linear_events \
  --compression zstd \
  --roll-interval 300 \
  --catalog-token YOUR_API_TOKEN
```

**3. Pipeline 作成**

```bash
npx wrangler pipelines create linear_events_pipeline \
  --sql-file sql/linear-pipeline.sql
```

**4. wrangler.jsonc の Stream ID を更新**

`REPLACE_WITH_STREAM_ID` を `6592c325ce2a4bc388840c1ea1eedeeb` に置き換え:

```bash
# 私が実行できます。やりますか？
```

**5. Worker デプロイ + シークレット設定**

```bash
pnpm deploy
wrangler secret put LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET
```

**6. Linear Webhook 設定**

Linear Settings > Administration > API > Webhooks で URL を設定

**対応する指摘:**
1. **JSON.parse の try/catch** (gemini, copilot, coderabbit — 3件共通) — malformed JSON で 500 ではなく 400 を返す
2. **署名比較の toLowerCase** (gemini) — hex 文字列の大文字小文字対応
3. **biome schema バージョン不一致** (coderabbit) — package.json を 2.4.11 に合わせる
4. **wrangler.jsonc の Stream ID** — 作成済みの ID に置換
5. **spec の Pulumi API 名** (coderabbit) — `r2.NewBucket` → `cloudflare.NewR2Bucket`

**対応しない指摘:**
- env バリデーション (gemini) — Workers のシークレットは設定済みなら必ず存在する。ランタイムチェックは不要
- テストで 401 のボディアサート (coderabbit nitpick) — 軽微、次回対応
- timingSafeEqual の長さ不一致時の早期リターン (copilot) — HMAC-SHA256 の出力は常に固定長なので実際には問題にならない
- happy-path 統合テスト (copilot) — Pipeline バインディングのモックが必要で、今回のスコープ外

1	import { Hono } from "hono";
2	import { verifySignature } from "../lib/signature";
3	import type { Env, LinearStreamEvent, LinearWebhookPayload } from "../types";
4	
5	const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS = 60_000;
6	
7	/**
8	 * Linear Webhook ペイロードを Stream スキーマに正規化する
9	 */
10	export function normalizePayload(payload: LinearWebhookPayload): LinearStreamEvent {
11	  return {
12	    action: payload.action,
13	    type: payload.type,
14	    created_at: payload.createdAt,
15	    webhook_id: payload.webhookId,
16	    webhook_timestamp: payload.webhookTimestamp,
17	    organization_id: payload.organizationId,
18	    url: payload.url ?? null,
19	    actor: payload.actor ?? null,
20	    data: payload.data,
21	    updated_from: payload.updatedFrom ?? null,
22	  };
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Webhook タイムスタンプが許容範囲内か検証する（リプレイ攻撃防止）
27	 */
28	export function validateTimestamp(webhookTimestamp: number): boolean {
29	  const now = Date.now();
30	  const diff = Math.abs(now - webhookTimestamp);
31	  return diff <= TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_MS;
32	}
33	
34	const linear = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();
35	
36	linear.post("/", async (c) => {
37	  const signature = c.req.header("Linear-Signature");
38	  if (!signature) {
39	    return c.json({ error: "Missing signature" }, 401);
40	  }
41	
42	  const rawBody = await c.req.text();
43	
44	  const isValid = await verifySignature(rawBody, signature, c.env.LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET);
45	  if (!isValid) {
46	    return c.json({ error: "Invalid signature" }, 401);
47	  }
48	
49	  const payload: LinearWebhookPayload = JSON.parse(rawBody);
50	
51	  if (!validateTimestamp(payload.webhookTimestamp)) {
52	    return c.json({ error: "Timestamp out of range" }, 401);
53	  }
54	
55	  const event = normalizePayload(payload);
56	
57	  await c.env.STREAM.send([event]);
58	
59	  return c.json({ success: true }, 200);
60	});
61	
62	export default linear;
63	
```

1	/**
2	 * 定数時間での文字列比較（タイミング攻撃防止）
3	 */
4	export function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
5	  if (a.length !== b.length) {
6	    return false;
7	  }
8	  let result = 0;
9	  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
10	    result |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
11	  }
12	  return result === 0;
13	}
14	
15	/**
16	 * Linear Webhook の HMAC-SHA256 署名を検証する
17	 */
18	export async function verifySignature(
19	  body: string,
20	  signature: string,
21	  secret: string
22	): Promise<boolean> {
23	  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
24	  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
25	    "raw",
26	    encoder.encode(secret),
27	    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
28	    false,
29	    ["sign"]
30	  );
31	  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, encoder.encode(body));
32	  const computed = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig))
33	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
34	    .join("");
35	
36	  return timingSafeEqual(computed, signature);
37	}
38

CI がまだ実行中です。Pulumi Preview と claude-review を待ちましょう。重大な問題がなければマージできる状態です。

**対応したレビューコメント:**
- `JSON.parse` の try/catch → 400 レスポンス (gemini, copilot, coderabbit 3件共通)
- 署名比較の `toLowerCase()` (gemini)
- Stream ID のプレースホルダー置換 (coderabbit)
- biome バージョン不一致 (coderabbit)
- spec の Pulumi API 名 (coderabbit)

**対応不要と判断:**
- env バリデーション — Workers Secrets は必ず存在する
- timingSafeEqual の長さ不一致 — HMAC は固定長
- happy-path 統合テスト — Pipeline モックが必要でスコープ外

> AGENT

マージ完了しました。 **PR #59** が main に squash merge されました。

---

**残りの手動タスク（デプロイ後）:**

1. **Pipelines Sink 作成** — R2 Catalog Token を Dashboard で作成後:
   ```bash
   npx wrangler pipelines sinks create linear_events_sink \
     --type r2-data-catalog --bucket lake --namespace streaming \
     --table linear_events --compression zstd --roll-interval 300 \
     --catalog-token $R2_CATALOG_TOKEN
   ```

2. **Pipeline 作成**:
   ```bash
   npx wrangler pipelines create linear_events_pipeline \
     --sql-file streaming/sql/linear-pipeline.sql
   ```

3. **Worker シークレット設定**: `wrangler secret put LINEAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET`

4. **Linear Webhook 設定**: URL を `https://streaming.<subdomain>.workers.dev/webhooks/linear` に設定

Pulumi Deploy の失敗原因は **D1 Database の `database_id` パラメータ欠落** で、今回の `lake` バケット追加とは無関係の既存の問題です（Cloudflare プロバイダのバージョンアップで D1 の API が変わった可能性）。

`lake` バケットは Wrangler で直接作成しましょう。Pulumi の問題は別 Issue で対応する形が良いと思います。

```bash
npx wrangler r2 bucket create lake --location apac
```

`lake` バケット作成完了です。Data Catalog も先ほど有効化済みなので、Sink 作成に進めます。

次のステップは:

1. **R2 Catalog Token 作成** — Cloudflare Dashboard で「R2 Admin Read & Write」権限のトークンを作成
2. **Sink → Pipeline 作成** — トークンを使って Wrangler CLI で実行
3. **Worker デプロイ + シークレット設定**
4. **Linear Webhook 設定**

トークンの作成は Dashboard で手動で行う必要があります。準備できたら教えてください。

Based on my investigation, here are my findings:

## Summary of Findings

**The Issue: D1Database Requiring `DatabaseId` in v6.14.0**

The error "missing required database_id parameter" suggests that the Pulumi Cloudflare provider v6.14.0 has introduced a breaking change requiring a `DatabaseId` field when creating a D1 Database resource.

### Key Findings:

1. **Current Code (cloudflare.go, lines 22-25):**
   - Your code only provides `AccountId` and `Name` when creating the D1Database
   - The comment on line 21 indicates this is meant to be an import of an **existing** database
   - No `DatabaseId` is being passed, which is now required in v6.14.0

2. **Provider Version:** 
   - You're using `github.com/pulumi/pulumi-cloudflare/sdk/v6 v6.14.0` (confirmed in go.mod)
   - Also have v5.49.1 installed but not actively used

3. **Pulumi.dev.yaml Configuration:**
   - Contains `accountId: b0047256d1afc1be1df08289ee3be552` configured
   - No D1 database ID is stored in the configuration

4. **Deployment Workflow:**
   - GitHub Actions workflow (`pulumi-deploy.yml`) uses `pulumi up --yes --refresh` which triggers the error
   - The workflow is correctly configured with necessary environment variables and R2 backend login

5. **Root Cause:**
   - For **importing an existing D1 database**, you need to either:
     - Add the `DatabaseId` field to `D1DatabaseArgs` with your existing database's ID, OR
     - Use `pulumi import` first to register the existing resource (as the comment suggests), then manage it without specifying DatabaseId

   - If you're trying to **create a new D1 database**, the API may have changed and now requires explicitly passing the DatabaseId after creation

**File Paths:**
- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go` (lines 22-25)
- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/Pulumi.dev.yaml`
- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/.github/workflows/pulumi-deploy.yml`

**Next Steps:** You'll need to either obtain your existing D1 database ID and add it to the configuration, or first run `pulumi import cloudflare:index/d1Database:D1Database raw <account_id>/<database_id>` before deploying with Pulumi Up.
```

# Go SDK Example for D1Database

## Go Code Example

```go
example, err := cloudflare.NewD1Database(ctx, "d1databaseResource", &cloudflare.D1DatabaseArgs{
    AccountId:           pulumi.String("string"),
    Name:                pulumi.String("string"),
    Jurisdiction:        pulumi.String("string"),
    PrimaryLocationHint: pulumi.String("string"),
    ReadReplication: &cloudflare.D1DatabaseReadReplicationArgs{
        Mode: pulumi.String("string"),
    },
})
```

## D1DatabaseArgs Parameters

**Required:**
- `AccountId` - Account identifier tag
- `Name` - Database name

**Optional:**
- `Jurisdiction` - Location restriction ("eu" or "fedramp")
- `PrimaryLocationHint` - Preferred region for primary instance
- `ReadReplication` - Read replication configuration

## DatabaseId Parameter

No. `DatabaseId` is **not** a required parameter. The resource uses `AccountId` and `Name` as inputs. The system generates a `Uuid` (database identifier) as an output property.

## Recent Changes

The documentation shows version 6.14.0 published April 2, 2026. The page indicates this is "6.x, latest" with previous versions 5.49.1 and 4.16.0 available, but specific changelog details about what changed aren't provided in this content.
```

1	name: Pulumi Deploy
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    paths:
7	      - "infrastructure/pulumi/**"
8	      - ".github/workflows/pulumi-deploy.yml"
9	
10	env:
11	  CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
12	  CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
13	  AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID }}
14	  AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: ${{ secrets.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY }}
15	  PULUMI_CONFIG_PASSPHRASE: ${{ secrets.PULUMI_CONFIG_PASSPHRASE }}
16	  SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME }}
17	  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME }}
18	  SNOWFLAKE_USER: PULUMI_SVC
19	  SNOWFLAKE_AUTHENTICATOR: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY
20	  SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER: OIDC
21	  SNOWFLAKE_ROLE: PULUMI_ROLE
22	
23	jobs:
24	  deploy:
25	    name: Deploy
26	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
27	    environment: pulumi
28	    permissions:
29	      id-token: write
30	      contents: read
31	    defaults:
32	      run:
33	        working-directory: infrastructure/pulumi
34	
35	    steps:
36	      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
37	
38	      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5
39	        with:
40	          go-version: "1.24"
41	          cache-dependency-path: infrastructure/pulumi/go.sum
42	
43	      - name: Install dependencies
44	        run: go mod tidy
45	
46	      - name: Get Snowflake OIDC token
47	        run: |
48	          OIDC_TOKEN=$(curl -sS -H "Authorization: bearer $ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN" \
49	            "${ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_URL}&audience=snowflakecomputing.com" | jq -r '.value')
50	          if [ -z "$OIDC_TOKEN" ] || [ "$OIDC_TOKEN" = "null" ]; then
51	            echo "::error::Failed to retrieve Snowflake OIDC token"
52	            exit 1
53	          fi
54	          echo "::add-mask::$OIDC_TOKEN"
55	          echo "SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN=$OIDC_TOKEN" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
56	
57	      - name: Login to R2 backend
58	        run: pulumi login 's3://pulumi?endpoint=https://${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com&region=auto&s3ForcePathStyle=true'
59	
60	      - name: Select stack
61	        run: pulumi stack select dev
62	
63	      - name: Pulumi Up
64	        run: pulumi up --yes --refresh
65	
```

> AGENT

`pulumi up --yes --refresh` の `--refresh` が原因で D1 の state リフレッシュ時にエラーが発生しています。

問題を整理します：

1. **D1 エラー**: provider v6.14.0 のリフレッシュ時に Cloudflare API が `database_id` を要求しているが、state に正しく保存されていない可能性
2. **`lake` バケット**: 手動作成済みだが Pulumi state にない → `pulumi up` で重複作成エラーになる

まず D1 の状態を確認し、ローカルで `pulumi import` して state を修正する必要があります。

> AGENT

D1 は state にある（id: `30fb49b7-...`）が、`lake` バケットは state にありません。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- D1 の refresh エラーは、Cloudflare provider v6.14.0 が API コール時に `database_id` をパスパラメータに必要とするが、state の `inputs` に含まれていない可能性。provider のバグか、state の形式が古い
- `lake` バケットは state に存在しないので、`pulumi up` で create しようとして「既に存在する」エラーになる
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

まず `lake` バケットを import して、D1 の問題を詳しく調べます。
